# B0249 — Gathering and being understood

A shared occasion can bring people together without making their choices, motives or accounts identical. B0249 follows Nanasta's Christmas party through its opening and reported aftermath, then turns to a New Year song-review broadcast and the walk recalled in Snow and Maiden Hearts Are Still Far. Christmas supplies enacted gathering and particular contributions while preserving resistance to future obligations. The broadcast makes songs and rival encounters into a public account without replaying their performances or closing the larger event. The walk lets companions negotiate choice, care and self-presentation, then tests imagined understanding against material and bodily consequences. These are source-local developments, not one synchronized calendar, a universal cure or a release-readiness result.

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0249-001 — Preparation becomes an enacted gathering**

The explicit party-day narration and final collective toast cross the earlier preparation horizon. The crossing occurs through further work, attention to an absent participant, an arrival and an offscreen transition. Coney's superlative is a festive aspiration, not a narrator-certified comparison with every party.

- **Shizuka → Musubi:** Requests party-day leniency, which Musubi accepts in the supplied exchange; not a permanent release from obligations. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0193.json__248be34140c60813?doc=primary&log=61#/Pages/61`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0193.json__248be34140c60813?doc=primary&log=63#/Pages/63`.
- **Coney → Musubi:** Adds an appeal for smiles during the party; Musubi's yielding is locally shared with Shizuka's request, not unconditional approval of lateness. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0193.json__248be34140c60813?doc=primary&log=62#/Pages/62`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0193.json__248be34140c60813?doc=primary&log=63#/Pages/63`.

#### Native presentation and actually reviewed stills

- All75complete native objects reconstructed byte-structurally through the lossless value/page projection; every field, value, absence and page assignment acquired in eight native parts in addition to six complete compact-text parts.
- Command-only P0/P1 use AutoMoveFrame40, bg_03→bg_086, adv_flash_white and the exact Title/SubTitle assets; two titleVoiceFileName cues and se_007 are metadata references, not heard sounds.
- BgmFileName bgm_005 begins P2; bgm_008 begins P66. SeFileName se_015 P10, se_013 P47 and se_030 P73 are exact carrier references. adv_shake atP34/P69/P71/P73; slidein_blackP65/slideout_blackP66; fadeout_whiteP74. No runtime cadence or motion observed.
- Charas arrays use seven slots with active assignments in slots1/3/5 and null surrounding slots. Spaces atP41/P66 are explicit non-asset clearing markers with IsFront/IsSpeaker/IsTremble false. No choice, branch, Item or movie field occurs in this complete case.
- AtP36 the literal compound Sumire & Susu has one dialogue/voice carrier while all displayed portraits' IsSpeaker flags are false. At collective P50/P69/P71/P73 portraits likewise cannot resolve individual chorus membership. Coney remains visible while collective responses occur.
- All52occurring sprite resources and2backgrounds hash-bound and actually reviewed; the Christmas-decorated room supports party framing, but the room art contains no visible active snowfall. Snow observation is textually attributed; a static room background does not negate it.
- Title assets visibly read EX EPISODE and Merry Christmas Nanasta concluding part; native file1193op name alone is not interpreted as opening-part semantics. Clothes-change instruction is not converted into a complete visual wardrobe change.

- All52labelledcomposites visible in full contact: Coney pink outfit/glasses and varied gestures, Haru school outfit, Musubi blue-green school clothing and changing mouth/eyelid forms, Rona scarf/casual clothing, Hime blue apron, Momoka pink jacket, Sumire cream jacket, Sui sailor outfit, Shizuka school outfit/long bag, Susu casual cardigan, Sawara and Kajika aprons, Shinju coat/shorts. Poses/expressions are static resources; no performed affect or animation inferred.
- bg_03 is a pale dotted light field. bg_086 is a festive common room with decorated Christmas trees, balloons, garlands, presents and furnishings. No snowfall is depicted in the static room image; snow is attributed text evidence.
- Black slide commands separate clothing instruction from the toast, but familiar Coney portraits do not establish all clothes actually changed. P36compoundandP50/P69/P71/P73collective labels are not resolved through speaker flags.

Haru asks whether that means learning a new hidden act; Player exclaims in surprise. Her interpretation is voiced as a question, not an agreed standing rule. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=47#/Pages/47` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=58#/Pages/58` (complete P47-58 context).
- **EV-B0249-015 / P59-65:** Momoka calls another act troublesome; Shinju firmly opposes making hidden acts customary; Musubi says remembering gives her a headache. Susu strongly supports the proposal only if she can receive a present from Player. Player says she is too close. Sumire asks whether he is pleased and he denies that reading as a misunderstanding. The text establishes competing interpretations and a proximity objection, not mutual romantic agreement or an accepted gift obligation. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=59#/Pages/59` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=65#/Pages/65` (complete P59-65 context).

Player responds with surprise. Haru comments that things end up this way and Coney calls this the current Nanasta. Player cries that he wants no more Christmas; native shake and white fade precede the final 終わり SubTitle. #### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0249-006 — Ellipsis and retrospective validation**

The family deliberately withholds the central festivities as directly staged action and gives their aftermath through selected participants' accounts. New claims about the duo's dance/song/costumes, illusion effort, hosting, gifts and snow are legitimate new testimony at this boundary. They must not be back-projected into earlier preparation freezes or converted into detailed witnessed mechanisms. Agreement about a broad enjoyable party is compatible with unknown individual acts, exact audience composition and delivery.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=1#/Pages/1`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20` (complete P13-20 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36` (complete P24-36 context).

**CL-B0249-007 — Uneven labor and membership**

Practical closure is distributed among kitchen and decoration workers, while credit for effort is disputed. Musubi challenges Momoka/Sawara in the immediate cleanup frame; Shinju mediates rather than disproves her observation. Sumire thanks Kajika/Shinju, and Shinju links contribution to Nanasta membership. That chosen contribution coexists with Shinju's later refusal to institutionalize hidden acts. Belonging therefore does not entail unrestricted availability or consent to every activity.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=2#/Pages/2` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12` (complete P2-12 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27` (complete P24-27 context).

**CL-B0249-008 — Joint making and the right to name**

Sui's praise opens public recognition of Haru and Kajika's bounded collaboration, but the pair themselves restore its complete name and insist on the small qualifier. Rona's aesthetic appraisal, Kajika's enjoyment of making with Haru, Player's reported reception and Coney's possibility appraisal are different evidence roles. Their convergence supports local recognition of joint work, not an objective quality ranking, permanent unit promotion or proof that the pair originally selected one another.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36` (complete P28-36 context).

**CL-B0249-009 — Wholehearted play has a cost question**

Player raises year-end workload; Coney answers with an ethic of wholehearted play and then work, echoed by Shizuka's attributed grandfather maxim. The source does not dissolve the burden question: Momoka withholds enthusiastic work assent, Shinju opposes a future hidden-act tradition and Musubi registers aversion at the memory. Coney's ethic should be read alongside these local limits rather than turned into narrator-endorsed unanimous institutional policy.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=46#/Pages/46` (complete P37-46 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=57#/Pages/57` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=62#/Pages/62` (complete P57-62 context).

**CL-B0249-010 — Different scopes of costume provision**

Haru/Kajika describe making their costumes together, while Haru later reports broad grandfather provision and tentatively infers its motive from Shizuka's quoted maxim. Retain both source statements and their scopes. The source does not enumerate which costumes, materials, other supplied items or financing arrangements belong to whom; neither all-self-made nor all-grandfather-made is warranted for the entire party.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=39#/Pages/39`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40`.

**CL-B0249-011 — Future belonging remains negotiable**

Player's warm next-year proposal immediately attracts different readings: Haru asks about new acts, Momoka complains, Shinju refuses a customary obligation, Musubi recalls strain, Susu sets a gift condition, and others name wishes. Player's denial of Sumire's romantic appraisal and his final outcry prevent a smooth consensus ending. Haru and Coney frame the contested ensemble as familiar/current Nanasta; these are their appraisals, not proof that every boundary has been negotiated or every desire reciprocated.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=51#/Pages/51` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0194.json__06fe2a186f5efe5c?doc=primary&log=72#/Pages/72` (complete P51-72 context).

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0249-016 — Broadcast makes plurality into a public account**

The story's action is an audience-facing review rather than a performed medley. Haru/Kajika organize institutional repertoire while Hime/Sui both host and interrupt. Artist commentary moves among recalled work, preferences, quoted conversations, shared jokes and audience invitations. Preserve whose interpretation is offered: Sui's plural SiSH liking, Kajika's Sawara quotation, Haru's Musubi/Kyoko reports and Hime's fan-response account do not grant direct access to every offscene subject.

**CL-B0249-017 — Competence includes managing interruptions**

Kajika's early entrance disrupts the planned introduction but Hime re-routes the segment after correction/apology. Haru reports practice with interviews without becoming perfectly controlled: the cue-card error exposes backstage writing, and a later offscene absence threatens the next segment. Hime's insistence that Sui stay at her host post contrasts stage continuity with presumed managerial pursuit. The comedy does not establish a safe production procedure, consent to the solo act or verified offstage labor.

Audience participation remains requested/anticipated in several passages. The closing resolution is formed through actual local proposals and replies rather than inferred from the larger unit name, and it does not audit the reception, labor or choice of all twelve members.

**CL-B0249-020 — Public affection does not settle temporary-unit status**

The pair jointly restores the small qualifier, explicitly locates the project in Christmas and reports Coney's uncertainty about continuation. Their enjoyment and wish for a future public live are compatible with that uncertainty. Hime's local praise and the displayed duo graphic support the presentation of UNIT-0033 but do not promote it to permanent governance or let later wishes rewrite the prior bounded Christmas collaboration.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0195.json__251c7583f1bbbc8d?doc=primary&log=99#/Pages/99` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0195.json__251c7583f1bbbc8d?doc=primary&log=115#/Pages/115` (complete P99-115 context).

**CL-B0249-021 — Formation through rivals without absorption**

A script error expands the song-review boundary; Haru then deliberately extends it to a rival because she treats confrontation as formative memory and gratitude. Sui recasts admiration through swimming rivalry: appreciation motivates stronger effort rather than erasing competition. Hime moves from objection to qualified competitive affirmation. 4U/KARAKURI remain distinct groups; the present speakers' accounts do not establish those groups' own new agreement, a cover license or resolved historical grievances.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0195.json__251c7583f1bbbc8d?doc=primary&log=125#/Pages/125` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0195.json__251c7583f1bbbc8d?doc=primary&log=142#/Pages/142` (complete P125-142 context).

**CL-B0249-022 — The review is an enacted interpretive practice**

The characters do more than list titles: they build metaphors, disagree about affect, disclose rehearsal anxieties, compare interpretations and invite shared gestures. LeSca's bright/dark framing is mediated by current hosts and Haru's attributed account, while Sui's ice-cream analogy makes interpretation itself part of banter. Rona's reported humming and dance teaching show music circulating through peer accounts, without replacing actual source listening or proving technical mastery.

**CL-B0249-023 — Source closure and event closure differ**

The singleton source completes its song-review segment but explicitly leaves the larger live in progress and the Musubi segment unresolved. A source-level family freeze can therefore close the admitted163-page document while retaining open diegetic obligations. Hime's offscene flight report is new evidence at this horizon; it must not be conflated with Musubi's earlier Christmas quitting announcement or used retrospectively to establish that older departure.

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0249-028 — Shared activity and refused self-entitlement**

Tomoe does not deny the concrete increase in talking and acting together; she disputes her entitlement to call it closeness through self-deprecation. Player supplies a rival interpretation grounded in that behavior. Retain both without assigning Tomoe a clinical condition, treating her denial as the narrator's truth or erasing her earlier chosen leadership and self-authorship. Catalog order does not diagnose regression.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15` (complete P8-15 context).

**CL-B0249-029 — Private recollection becomes potential publicity**

The magazine request turns a leisure walk into a search for a public account of shared work. Mimi selects a salient ordinary memory, then questions its suitability under an anticipated male reception. Her final filter shows an imagined audience shaping potential self-presentation, while no actual editor/reader reaction or chosen interview story is supplied.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23` (complete P18-23 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=41#/Pages/41`.

**CL-B0249-030 — Care includes the other person's choice**

Mimi's intended suitability is qualified by her own admission that buying for both may have overreached and that personal choice matters. Tomoe's later reassurance rests partly on self-doubt; Saori's explanation is bodily exclusion from the choice process. These are distinct receptions, not equivalent advance assent. Mimi's invitation to choose/show clothes together opens a more reciprocal possibility, but its completion remains unshown.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=34#/Pages/34` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=39#/Pages/39` (complete P34-39 context).

**CL-B0249-031 — Enthusiasm and bodily cost coexist**

Mimi frames bargain shopping through discernment, competition and satisfaction; Tomoe admires the unfamiliar practice. Saori's reported trampling/infirmary visit interrupts any harmless universal account, yet she herself wants to retry and learn. Preserve that agency without turning harm into a benefit, proving safe practice or inferring a current injury from her familiar bandaged character art.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=39#/Pages/39` (complete P27-39 context).

**CL-B0249-032 — Older-sister role stays particular and fallible**

Mimi asks companions' preferences, describes a specific enthusiasm, questions her overreach and welcomes Saori's wish to learn. The role can support shared experience while being revised by another person's choice; it is neither a universal taste authority nor literal kinship. Tomoe's awakening metaphor is answered as exaggeration and need not become a new world power.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=2#/Pages/2`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40` (complete P27-40 context).

**CL-B0249-033 — Gendered reception remains a speaker's anticipation**

Mimi's interview hesitation concerns how she expects men to receive bargain talk. It can constrain her narration here without proving an objective audience law, all male preferences, a management ban or a final editorial decision. A rival reading is practical topic selection rather than complete suppression; the chapter leaves selection unresolved.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0196.json__0958a6bc957bce28?doc=primary&log=41#/Pages/41`.

Saori describes enjoying wind and snow. Mimi then asks whether enjoyment is the only reason Saori wants practice; Saori is startled and Tomoe notices. These are attributed recollections and a counterfactual, not a directly staged collision or a verified medical outcome. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18` (complete P13-18 context).

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0249-034 — Pleasure and responsibility are simultaneous motives**

Mimi's question elicits the concern that skill might prevent involving Player, alongside Saori's already stated sensory enjoyment. This does not invalidate the pleasure or reduce practice to concealed guilt alone. Saori's own counterfactual gives the practice an ethical aim while preserving her distinctive willingness to fall; it also exposes why reassurances about Player's feelings do not answer her principle.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=4#/Pages/4` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19` (complete P4-19 context).

**CL-B0249-035 — A principle meets the practical problem it does not solve by naming**

Yamaism articulates Saori's refusal to transfer her costs to others. Tomoe both admires the label and asks whether calling it an ism supplies a solution. Her later self-correction about positivity keeps the exchange from certifying resilient enthusiasm as automatically safe. Saori's pro-level aspiration is a desired means of protecting others, not an attained technical competence or permission to neglect her own bodily cost.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26` (complete P19-26 context).

**CL-B0249-036 — Shared care contests unilateral containment of risk**

Mimi counters Saori's proposed self-containment with accompanying and catching. Saori contributes information about how often she falls, rather than simply accepting a rescue script. The exchange supports a negotiation over how care is distributed; it does not prove Mimi's forecast of Player's response or the efficacy of catching. A rival reading is that Mimi's confidence underestimates the warning. Preserve that unresolved practical limit alongside her concrete offer.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31` (complete P27-31 context).

**CL-B0249-037 — The recipient of care retains a speaking position**

Saori rejects one reassurance, hesitates before the next proposal, warns Mimi about frequency and ends with a questioning response to Tomoe's new behavior. These responses prevent group enthusiasm from becoming blanket assent, delight or a settled care plan. Her willingness to learn and invite companionship coexists with a boundary about involving others; it is not a fixed diagnosis of isolation.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=42#/Pages/42`.

**CL-B0249-038 — Private role aspiration becomes public agreement through different evidence**

Tomoe's parenthesized thought makes admiration for Mimi a motive available to the reader. Her surprise, laughter and later spoken agreement are the evidence available to Mimi and Saori. The older-sister address lets Tomoe imagine a valued place in the group and choose participation despite her earlier preference for indoors. It establishes neither literal kinship nor permanent replacement of her preferences, earlier leadership or self-authorship.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=38#/Pages/38` (complete P32-38 context).

**CL-B0249-039 — Reciprocity unsettles a single competent caretaker hierarchy**

Mimi offers supervision and invites Tomoe into the role, but then asks Saori to teach Yamaism. Saori is both someone to accompany and someone from whom the older two may learn. Tomoe's willingness is partly aspirational, while Saori's final uncertainty resists a seamless performance of authority. The chapter extends Mimi's particular, fallible older-sister practice rather than appointing a universal expert or forming a new formal unit.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40` (complete P29-40 context).

**CL-B0249-040 — Unequal recollections must remain unequal**

Impact, Player's appearance of pain, Tomoe's explanation of fainting as surprise and Mimi's later generalization that Saori does not let him get hurt are different evidential forms. None alone settles a medical outcome; combining them into either proven injury or proven harmlessness would exceed the scene. The central question is how characters interpret responsibility and reassurance, not a clinical verdict supplied by the narration.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18` (complete P13-18 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0197.json__971aee54d19c89f4?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24`.

#### Source-local character and collective changes

- **Saori / CHAR-0038:** Enjoys the mountain activity and wants practice while explicitly aiming not to involve others in her falls. #### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0249-041 — Permission to try meets imagined exclusion**

Tomoe treats ordinary clothes shopping as a space her presence might harm, whereas stage costume is an exception she can imagine. Mimi counters with possible outfits and Saori with a shared invitation. This is evidence of competing self/appraisals and a locally offered opportunity, not an actual shop's exclusion or objective absence of attractiveness. Mimi's girl-before-idol rationale is her normative framing, not a universal gender rule or a requirement that Tomoe dress a particular way.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=1#/Pages/1` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8` (complete P1-8 context).

**CL-B0249-042 — Saori's hesitation protects the clothes as well as herself**

Saori expresses interest before worrying that injury would soil a garment, then recruits Tomoe into trying. The familiar bodily-risk motif takes the unusual direction of concern for the clothes. It does not establish an inevitable accident, safety clearance or refusal of fashion. Her invitation makes her an active peer rather than merely Mimi's dependent.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=3#/Pages/3`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`.

**CL-B0249-043 — Being found supplies attention without omniscience**

Tomoe's hidden snow-making and Mimi's relay of Player's report offer a counterpoint to her account of invisibility. Player's finding is mediated here through her and Mimi, while scouting genius is Mimi's praise. Neither the where-ever generalization nor that praise proves supernatural tracking, omniscience, an entitlement to intrude or an independently confirmed motive. The earlier snow-hut occasions retain their separate source formulations.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=9#/Pages/9` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15` (complete P9-15 context).

**CL-B0249-044 — A new side is proposed, not discovered by the analysis**

Mimi recasts an encounter in a flirtation script; Tomoe considers showing a new side and rehearses speech. This shows experimentation with self-presentation through peer advice. It does not disclose a settled hidden identity or prove romantic reciprocity. Saori's echo is immediately challenged as uncomprehending, so apparent verbal agreement cannot become equal understanding of the advice.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26` (complete P16-26 context).

**CL-B0249-045 — Escalation exposes the limits of the adviser**

The conversation shifts from retrospective counterfactual to immediate logistical proposal. Mimi's interrupted assent, startled swimsuit response and objection about public suspicion place limits on her earlier confidence. Tomoe's enthusiasm extrapolates beyond the advice, while Mimi later questions having encouraged too much. A reading of growing initiative is available, but it must retain the impracticality and the adviser's uncertainty rather than certify a successful maturation.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33` (complete P27-33 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36`.

**CL-B0249-046 — Borrowed institutional power remains speculative**

Tomoe invokes Kuonji resources as a possible means to make a hut instantly. The utterance establishes her expectation and imaginative leap, not corporate participation, permission, a demonstrated snow-production technology or Shizuka/grandfather's own position. The narrow current-ledger literal lookup returned no matching collective reference; that bounded result is not evidence of global source absence and licenses no new corporate capability.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32` (complete P29-32 context).

**CL-B0249-047 — An older-sister role changes form across distinct moments**

Tomoe's rehearsed address echoes the valued older-sister role of the preceding chapter, now as a self-presentation experiment. Mimi's final rival appraisal introduces competition into her earlier supportive positioning. Keep these as local performances and an anticipated future, alongside reciprocal care and Tomoe's established authorship/leadership, rather than make one permanent hierarchy, literal kinship or a newly completed romantic relation.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=25#/Pages/25`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37` (complete P33-37 context).

**CL-B0249-048 — The final appraisal has a narrower audience**

After Tomoe and Saori are removed from the native staging, Mimi's self-questioning and future-rival expectation become evidence for the reader about her appraisal. They are not automatically messages received by the absent pair or Player. This distinction also keeps a departed conversation from becoming a completed construction adventure.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0198.json__802beae13aee0fd8?doc=primary&log=38#/Pages/38` (complete P33-38 context).

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0249-049 — An incidental work request can acquire a relational value**

Player describes the interview request as passing on work; Mimi values the conversation it prompted and feels closer to the others. The two accounts can coexist without proving he planned a therapeutic intervention or that all three now use the same friendship label. Mimi's gratitude records her interpretation and felt change, while Tomoe's earlier refusal of the label remains part of the family horizon.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=3#/Pages/3` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10` (complete P3-10 context).

**CL-B0249-050 — Closeness does not require full managerial access to the account**

Mimi withholds the intended interview material from Player and gives a gendered explanation. Player's questions show he has not acquired its contents or the park plan. This is a local asymmetry in disclosure, not evidence that no topic was chosen, that publication occurred, or that management has a documented entitlement or ban. Mimi's voiced worry also qualifies her earlier confidence as adviser.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24` (complete P11-24 context).

**CL-B0249-051 — The desired transformation meets its material substitute**

Tomoe names the white cardboard structure a snow hut, while Player attends to cardboard and a secret-base appearance. His mundane question forces her to compare result and expectation. Kuonji cooperation remains a speculative explanation for what was missing; there is no actual technological intervention. This makes the failure specific to the enacted plan rather than proving Tomoe inherently incapable of initiative or resetting her earlier character history.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33` (complete P28-33 context).

**CL-B0249-052 — The heated line becomes bodily need**

The repeated heat wording moves from the imagined persuasive line to Tomoe's feverish feeling, runny nose and inadequate shelter. Player responds with a tissue rather than the forecast romantic susceptibility. This textual pun and practical care are supported without hearing the delivery. A possible cold remains her own tentative judgment, and no medical outcome, swimsuit appearance or romantic acceptance is invented.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=34#/Pages/34` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37` (complete P34-37 context).

**CL-B0249-053 — The responsibility problem returns through action**

Saori's wish to practise without involving others now encounters a playground substitution and Player's danger response. Her concern for him and appeal to failed Yamaism show that responsibility remains unresolved in practice. They do not prove she lacks care, that injury is beneficial, that care must be refused, or that Mimi's promised catching was successfully implemented. The exact physical result stays qualified.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=39#/Pages/39` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=47#/Pages/47` (complete P39-47 context).

**CL-B0249-054 — Caring acts need not receive the intended meaning**

Tomoe attempts an appeal; Player offers mundane help. Saori tries practice and then worries about him. Mimi reads the gap as hearts not reaching Player. The sequence supports a mismatch between intended presentation and received event, but her closing romantic frame is not an omniscient account of all motives or reciprocal feelings. Practical attention remains real even when the imagined communication fails.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=50#/Pages/50` (complete P28-50 context).

**CL-B0249-055 — The older-sister adviser has knowledge and limits**

Mimi knows more of the park plan than Player, worries about the escalation and appears in the park framing, yet the source does not specify an exact supervision or rescue action. Her closing appraisal is particular to what she sees/interprets, not proof of comprehensive control or access to every interior. The family retains warmth, misjudgment, reciprocity and bodily cost without producing an infallible caretaker hierarchy.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23` (complete P17-23 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=49#/Pages/49`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0199.json__a953465427e5c6c8?doc=primary&log=50#/Pages/50`.

**CL-B0249-056 — Closure is narrative completion, not practical or release completion**

The explicit End closes the four-chapter family while leaving interview contents, medical recovery, future practice and mutual understanding unresolved. Its coda revises the earlier imagined success into a bounded failed communication. It neither retroactively fulfills chapter-one shopping plans or chapter-two accompaniment promises nor satisfies the full2034 analytical release.
